## License: unknown
https://github.com/ghcpuman902/manglekuo.com/blob/53eba8b24dc2b24f071872b5bd737959efa50a2f/tailwind.config.js

```
type { Config } from "tailwindcss";

export default {

  darkMode: ["class"],

  content: [

    './pages/**/*.{ts,tsx}',

    './components/**/*.{ts,tsx}',

    './app/**/*.{ts,tsx}',

    './src/**/*.{ts,tsx}',

  ],

  prefix: "",

  theme: {

    container: {

      center: true,

      padding: "2rem",

      screens: {

        "2xl": "1400px",

      },

    },

    extend: {

      colors: {

        border: "hsl(var(--border))",

        input: "hsl(var(--input))",

        ring: "hsl(var(--ring))",

        background: "hsl(var(--background))",
```
